*Benedikt J. Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种双轴校准策略，分别利用答案置信度和隐藏状态探针来区分模型应拒绝的两类情况：答错的问题和不可回答的问题（如虚假前提），并证明单一阈值无法区分，而双轴策略在多规模模型上显著提升拒绝精度和覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型拒绝机制通常依赖单一置信度阈值，无法区分“模型会答错”和“问题本身不可回答（如虚假前提、无答案）”，导致拒绝决策不精确，且随模型规模扩大该盲区不减反增。

Method: 在三个模型家族（2B-14B）的五个指令微调模型上实验，对比普通答案置信度与隐藏状态线性探针在正确性与可回答性检测上的表现；在CREPE虚假前提数据集上测试多种基线；提出基于双轴（可回答性得分与正确性得分）的校准拒绝策略，并与单阈值基线对比。

Result: 答案置信度擅长判断正确性但对可回答性近乎盲目，隐藏状态探针则相反；盲区随规模不缩减，在CREPE上各类显式置信度指标均接近随机，而隐藏状态探针达0.69-0.77 AUROC；直接指令模型检查前提会大量误报（57%虚假挑战），结合探针路由可将挑战精度约三倍；双轴策略在0.75正确答案覆盖率下同时认证两类拒绝预算，单阈值仅0.31，14B模型仅双轴策略可认证。

Conclusion: 模型内部表征了可回答性信息但不显式输出，需通过隐藏状态探针提取；双轴校准策略能有效分离两类拒绝原因，提供可控的不可回答拒绝率和由模型精度上界约束的错误拒绝率，随阈值收紧保证增强，且随模型规模扩大优势更显著，是大规模模型可靠拒绝的必要方案。

Abstract: A model should refuse two different things: answers it would get wrong, and questions it should not answer at all, such as unanswerable ones or ones resting on a false premise. The usual recipe thresholds a single confidence score, which cannot tell these apart. Across five instruction-tuned models from three families (2B to 14B), we find they are separate axes. Ordinary answer-confidence tracks whether an answer is right but is nearly blind to whether the question is answerable; a linear probe on hidden states does the reverse. The blind spot does not shrink with scale. It is worst on naturally occurring false-premise questions (CREPE). There, answer-confidence, P(IK), P(True), and even asking the model outright whether a premise is false all stay near chance, while a hidden-state probe reaches 0.69 to 0.77 AUROC: the model represents a problem it will not report. This turns out to be fixable. Instructing a model to check premises backfires, because it then disputes sound and false premises alike (57% false challenges), unable to tell them apart; routing the same instruction with the probe roughly triples challenge precision. We turn the two axes into a calibrated policy that answers only when an answerability score and a correctness score each clear a separately certifies behave differently: the unanswerable-answer rate is controllable at every scale, while the wrong-answer rate is capped by model accuracy, so the guarantee tightens as threshold policy certifies both budgets at 0.75 coverage of correct answers, against 0.31 for a single threshold; at 14B it is the only policy that certifies at all.

</details>


### [33] [Cross-seed explainability using Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08499)
*Bendegúz Váradi,Zoltán Kmetty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Procrustes条件的联合端到端Top-K稀疏自编码器，用于从独立训练的BERT模型中提取跨种子通用特征，解决了字典学习非凸性导致的特征空间错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机制可解释性中，跨种子特征通用性是一个基本挑战：由于字典学习是非凸的，独立训练的网络会学习到错位的特征空间，表面上相同的特征可能因随机初始化而不同。

Method: 计算种子间激活空间的正交Procrustes旋转，然后进行联合SAE训练，结合Top-K稀疏性、端到端下游优化，以及基于先前SAE文献的辅助死特征复活损失。

Result: 在五个独立种子对（十个BERT模型）和三个基准数据集（SST-2、Stanford Politeness、TweetEval Emotion）上评估，完整流程产生的通用特征（皮尔逊相关系数 ≥ 0.70）优于所有三个数据集上的事后对齐基线。定性分析证实高通用性特征编码了可解释的社会语言学模式。

Conclusion: 该方法通过Procrustes对齐和联合端到端训练有效提取了跨种子通用的可解释特征，为机制可解释性中的跨模型特征对齐提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a Procrustes-conditioned Joint End-to-end Top-K Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) for extracting cross-seed universal features from independently trained BERT models. Cross-seed feature universality is a fundamental challenge in mechanistic interpretability: because dictionary learning is non-convex, independently trained networks learn misaligned feature spaces, so apparently identical features may differ by random initialization. We address this by computing an orthogonal Procrustes rotation between seeds' activation spaces before joint SAE training, combining Top-K sparsity, end-to-end downstream optimization, and an auxiliary dead-feature revival loss based on previous SAE literature. Evaluating on five independent seed pairs (ten BERT models) across three benchmark datasets (SST-2, Stanford Politeness, TweetEval Emotion), our full pipeline produces more universal features (Pearson r $\geq$ 0.70 across seeds) than post-hoc alignment baselines on all three datasets. A minimal qualitative analysis confirms that high-universality features encode interpretable sociolinguistic patterns.

</details>


### [34] [When the Judge Changes, So Does the Measurement: Auditing LLM-as-Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08535)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou,Xiaokun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将评估者替换导致的评分波动视为测量效度问题，对比了两条升级路径：Qwen3密集模型从1.7B到32B的参数扩展，以及MiniMax M2到M2.7的API版本迭代。实验发现：仅Qwen3 1.7B→4B带来稳健的相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本无显著提升；更强的评估模型虽能减弱但无法消除位置偏差和冗长偏差；当错误相关时，重复采样陪审团作用有限；结构化辩论能显著改变判决，但缺乏解析器和回溯日志无法归因于深思熟虑。作者建议LLM-as-judge报告应包含数据切片、偏差探针、错误依赖估计和协议审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge评分会因评估模型更换而波动，即使候选回答固定不变。这种评估者替换歧义威胁测量效度，亟需系统研究不同升级路径对判决稳定性、偏差及误差结构的影响，以制定可靠的评估报告规范。

Method: 在四个判断数据集上，对比两条实际可用的升级路径：(1) Qwen3密集模型参数从1.7B扩展至32B；(2) MiniMax M2至M2.7发布版API的跨版本迁移。通过相邻版本对比、偏差探针（位置/冗长）、重复采样陪审团、结构化辩论实验，量化升级带来的判决变化、偏差残留、误差相关性及归因能力。

Result: 1. 升级路径不可互换：仅Qwen3 1.7B→4B给出稳健相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本无显著改进。2. 更强评估模型减弱但未消除位置偏差与冗长偏差。3. 错误相关时，重复采样陪审团边际收益极小。4. 结构化辩论能大幅翻转判决，但无解析器与回溯日志无法将变化归因于深思熟虑过程。

Conclusion: LLM-as-judge评估报告必须包含：数据切片分析、偏差探针测试、错误依赖估计、协议审计轨迹（解析器与回溯日志），以确保判决的可解释性、可复现性与测量效度。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [35] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAESTRO 是一种面向稀疏激活 MoE 模型的结构化剪枝框架，利用遍历马尔可夫链建模专家激活轨迹，捕捉跨层路由依赖，实现全局感知的重要性评估；在 50% 压缩率下，其平均性能保持率比最强基线高出 10.61%，且跨任务方差显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏激活的 MoE 语言模型虽然推理高效，但完整专家库常驻内存造成部署瓶颈；现有针对密集 Transformer 的结构化剪枝方法依赖局部启发式评估专家重要性，忽视了 MoE 路由机制的相互依赖性。

Method: 提出 MAESTRO（基于马尔可夫链近似的专家稀疏化框架），将自回归生成过程中的专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用其平稳分布编码跨层路由依赖，得到全局感知的专家重要性启发式，进而执行路由一致的结构化剪枝。

Result: 在安全、偏见、伦理等五个多样化领域评测，MAESTRO 在严格 50% 压缩制度下，平均性能保持率超越最强基线达 10.61%，并表现出更低的跨任务方差。

Conclusion: 全局且与路由机制一致的剪枝策略能生成在异构任务上泛化更一致的模型。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [36] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DominoTree，一种无需训练的基于最优先搜索的推测解码树方法，结合了 Domino 的路径相关非因子化修正与 CUDA 原生树构建器，在多个基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的块扩散起草器（如 DFlash）仅建模逐位置边缘分布，而最优先树方法（如 DDTree）基于这些边缘分布扩展候选树，无法表示 Domino 引入的 GRU 因果修正所带来的路径相关条件分布。需要一种能利用 Domino 的非因子化路径修正且计算高效的树搜索方法。

Method: DominoTree 采用无训练的最优先搜索策略，沿每条根到节点路径使用 Domino 的条件非因子化修正为草稿树评分，通过限制每节点修正仅在候选 top-M 中计算以保持实用性。同时设计了 GPU 原生的 CUDA 图构建器，实现与 Python 参考实现逐比特一致的树构建，保证接受率不变且单轮构建开销低。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B 上跨八个基准测试，DominoTree 达到高达 6.6 倍的自回归解码加速，平均接受长度最高达 10.7 token/轮，在所有测试温度下均保持最优。相比发布版 Domino 解码器，总体吞吐率提升 9-10%（Alpaca 上 +22%）；在所有温度下均超越 DDTree/CaDDTree。在 Qwen3-8B 上，同样在所有温度下保持最高接受长度，在 T=0 时比 DDTree 提升 24% 吞吐率，高温下优势缩小至持平或微弱劣势，但总体聚合指标仍持续优于 DFlash 和 Domino。

Conclusion: DominoTree 成功将 Domino 的路径相关非因子化修正集成到最优先树搜索中，并配合高效的 CUDA 图构建器，实现了最先进的推测解码性能，在多种模型规模和温度设置下均表现出稳健的加速效果和最高的接受长度。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [37] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 UltraX，一种面向大规模预训练数据的函数调用式精炼框架，引入插入、删除、修改三种编辑操作，实现细粒度实例级编辑；构建程序监督生成流水线，结合数据集自适应提示优化、行对齐映射、动态上下文替换、低置信度样本过滤、比例控制采样、滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合及系统化后处理，显著提升大规模数据精炼的质量、效率与可靠性。实验表明 UltraX 在所有语料上平均性能最高，且以更少训练令牌达到或超越基线，展现更强的数据效率与精炼可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可用训练数据逼近物理极限，Scaling Laws 的收益递减，提升大语言模型更依赖高质量数据利用而非数据扩展。现有精炼方法在大规模语料下面临质量、效率、可靠性的三重瓶颈：基于规则的方法受限于固定启发式，难以处理实例级变异；基于 LLM 的方法虽提升质量，但难以满足大规模处理的效率与可靠性需求。

Method: UltraX 通过函数调用框架补全编辑操作空间（插入、删除、修改），实现细粒度实例级编辑。核心流水线包括：1) 数据集自适应提示优化，引导专家 LLM 生成高质量端到端精炼文本；2) 行对齐映射与动态上下文替换，将原文-精炼文本对转化为结构化程序监督；3) 低置信度样本过滤与操作组合比例控制采样，提升监督质量并稳定训练分布；4) 推理执行阶段采用滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合与系统化后处理，规范化并验证模型输出，增强大规模执行的稳定性与可靠性。

Result: 实验显示 UltraX 在所有测试语料上平均性能最高，且在使用更少训练令牌的情况下达到或超越基线方法，证明其具备更强的数据效率与精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX 通过完善编辑操作空间、构建可靠的程序监督生成流水线以及强化推理执行的规范化验证机制，有效解决了大规模预训练数据精炼中质量、效率、可靠性难以兼顾的问题，为数据高效利用提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [38] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm 是一种渐进式递归委托框架，通过动态实例化智能体搜索节点，结合任务分解、递归扩展和智能体协作，显著提升了基于 LLM 的网页搜索在深度、广度及深广交织任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单智能体 ReAct 模式受限于单一长轨迹和有限上下文，难以兼顾搜索深度与覆盖面；现有多智能体系统虽通过并行执行和聚合提升覆盖，但在递归深度、协作自适应性和证据驱动扩展方面仍有明显局限。

Method: 提出 WebSwarm 框架：在推理期联合构建任务分解、递归扩展和智能体协作。动态实例化智能体搜索节点，每个节点耦合局部目标与搜索模式，可自行求解或进一步委托子节点；求解后向上返回证据与结果，支撑父节点进一步扩展、修正或聚合。先探测任务相关信息在网页上的组织方式以奠定节点扩展基础，并在同质兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch 和 GISA 四个基准上，WebSwarm 在深度、广度及深广交织任务上均持续超越单智能体和多智能体基线。消融实验、任务难度、网页工具效率及模型泛化性分析进一步解释了其有效性并为多智能体搜索系统提供洞见。

Conclusion: WebSwarm 通过渐进式递归委托机制，有效解决了复杂网页搜索任务中深度与广度难以兼顾的问题，为构建更强大的多智能体搜索系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [39] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在深度研究系统中使用LLM裁判员评估引文质量的校准问题，发现较便宜的模型（如GPT-5-mini）在源相关性和事实支持性两个维度上均具有竞争力，且无单一模型全面占优；标量F1掩盖了方向性偏差（通过率漂移、假阳性/假阴性率差异），而这些正是强化学习循环会强化的内容，因此裁判员校准是将引文评分用作奖励信号的前提，且不需要最昂贵的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习越来越依赖LLM裁判员对各评分标准打分作为奖励模型，但在信任该信号前，必须明确裁判员需要具备何种能力及其偏差程度。本文聚焦深度研究系统中的引文质量评估，这是一个结构化评分任务，需判断源相关性与事实支持性两个维度。

Method: 在对抗性长文本基准上，选取3个模型家族的8个现成LLM裁判员，对照经过人工复核的1,248个评分决策（含378个由裁判员分歧裁定的困难案例）进行评测，对比各模型在源相关性与事实支持性两个维度上的表现，并分析通过率漂移、假阳性率、假阴性率等方向性偏差指标。

Result: GPT-5-mini在源相关性上达到最强通过类F1（0.908，κ=0.636）；在事实支持性上各裁判员统计上无显著差异（置信区间重叠），无单一模型主导。在相近F1下，各裁判员在通过率漂移、假阳性率、假阴性率上差异显著，标量F1掩盖了这些方向性偏差。

Conclusion: 裁判员校准是将引文评分用作强化学习奖励信号的必要前提，且该校准不需要最昂贵的模型；较便宜的模型即可提供具有竞争力的评分质量，但需显式量化并校正其方向性偏差。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [40] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文测试葡萄牙国家语言模型AMALIA在道德基础权威编码任务上的效度，发现其整体表现虽与人类编码员一致，但分解提示后性能大幅下降，表明模型依赖表面相关性而非理论构建；多语言模型在同一语料上表现更好，提示主权LLM基准应测试证据路径而非仅一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家语言模型为语言社区提供测量公民言论和价值观的工具，但仅靠与人类编码员的一致性（可靠性）不足以保证测量效度；需要检验模型是否遵循理论构建还是利用捷径。

Method: 采用“恢复差距”测试，将整体提示分解为代码本的原子子句并按理论显式规则重组，比较分解后的性能损失；测试校准后的英语工具能否迁移到AMALIA-9B和欧洲葡萄牙语；对比开源多语言LLM在同一语料上的表现。

Result: 对于一个构建和一个语料，校准英语工具未能迁移；分解仅恢复AMALIA整体性能约一半，误差分析显示依赖表面相关性（如权威人物附近的道德愤怒）；开源多语言LLM在相同条件下弥补了差距，表明问题不在语料。

Conclusion: AMALIA可用于大规模筛选和预编码，但尚不能独立良好测量该构建；单一反例不代表否定国家模型，但主权LLM基准测试应不仅考察与人类编码员的一致性，还应考察支撑一致性的证据路径。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [41] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench是首个面向动态真实环境的主动智能体能力驱动基准，围绕五大核心能力设计400个双语任务，采用实时Docker容器分步检查点评估，并引入闭环评估策略模拟多轮人类反馈，揭示基座模型能力与智能体框架设计对性能的共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以有效评估主动智能体，主要局限于沙盒环境、单轮评估范式，且基于场景的任务分类混合多种模型能力，难以定位智能体失败的根本原因。

Method: 基于五大基础能力（技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解、跨平台协调）构建400个双语真实任务；在实时Docker容器中采用细粒度分步完成检查点评估；设计包含执行智能体、隐藏监督智能体和用户智能体的闭环评估策略，模拟真实多轮人类反馈且不泄露评分标准；在多个智能体框架下评估最先进模型，以区分基座模型能力与框架级设计选择。

Result: 通过跨模型和框架的全面对比，展示了基座模型能力和智能体框架设计如何共同塑造真实环境中的性能。

Conclusion: UniClawBench为主动智能体在动态真实环境中的评估提供了新范式，基准和代码已公开，将促进未来研究。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>


### [42] [Can We Trust LLM's Logic? Quantifying Uncertainty, Coherence, and Robustness via a Graph-Based Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08017)
*Riccardo Revalor,Jalees Rehman,Debjit Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAPHEVAL框架，将LLM推理的不确定性量化重构为整体推理保真度问题，引入图推理连贯性分数（GRCS）和基于类中心的图自我一致性（GSC）解码策略，实验表明GRCS在不同规模模型上均与推理忠实度呈负相关，GSC在牺牲名义准确率的情况下提升推理保真度，且对抗性类中心消融实验揭示GSC选定路径为“承重路径”，偏离该路径会显著降低推理忠实度和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码策略如Self-Consistency（SC）仅评估最终答案一致性，忽略中间推理步骤的逻辑有效性，无法可靠量化推理不确定性，也无法检测病态模式崩溃和自信幻觉。需解决三个核心问题：如何可靠量化LLM推理不确定性？语义、结构、因果感知能否比朴素多数投票选择更忠实的推理？推理拓扑在对抗条件下有多鲁棒？

Method: 提出GRAPHEVAL图推理框架：将推理过程建模为图结构，节点为推理步骤，边为语义/结构/因果关系。设计GRCS指标，量化推理空间的语义-结构共识，捕捉模式崩溃和自信幻觉。提出GSC解码策略，基于类中心选择推理路径，以推理保真度换取名义准确率。通过对抗性类中心消融实验，验证GSC选定路径的承重作用。

Result: GRCS是唯一在更强模型和较小模型上均与推理忠实度持续负相关的指标。GSC在较小模型中暴露SC被不忠实幸运猜测虚高的准确率，在更强模型中保持或提升准确率。对抗性消融实验表明，强制模型偏离GSC选定路径会降低推理忠实度，并在针对性情况下导致准确率下降，证明该路径为“承重路径”。

Conclusion: 基于图的推理框架能整体量化推理不确定性，GRCS提供了可靠的推理保真度度量，GSC解码策略有效平衡了准确率与推理忠实度，且推理拓扑存在关键承重路径，偏离该路径会严重损害推理质量，为提升LLM推理可靠性提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large-Language Models (LLMs) can be prone to flawed and unfaithful reasoning that decoding strategies like Self-Consistency (SC) fail to detect as they evaluate only final-answer agreement while ignoring the logical validity of intermediate steps. This raises three fundamental questions: How can we reliably quantify uncertainty in LLM reasoning? Can semantic, structural, and causal awareness select more faithful reasoning compared to naïve majority voting? and How robust is reasoning topology under adversarial conditions? To address these questions, we introduce GRAPHEVAL, a graph-based reasoning framework that re-frames uncertainty quantification (UQ) as a holistic reasoning fidelity problem. We propose a novel UQ metric, Graph Reasoning Coherence Score (GRCS), that quantifies semantic-structural consensus of the reasoning space and captures pathological mode collapse and confident hallucinations. We find that GRCS is the only metric that is consistently negatively correlated with reasoning faithfulness across both more capable and smaller models. Additionally, we introduce Graph Self-Consistency (GSC), a medoid-based decoding strategy that trades nominal accuracy for reasoning fidelity, exposing the degree to which SC is inflated by unfaithful lucky guesses in smaller models, while preserving or improving accuracy in more capable ones. Finally, through adversarial medoid ablation, we demonstrate that the GSC-selected path acts as a "load-bearing path" and forcing models away from it degrades reasoning faithfulness and, in targeted cases, causes drops in accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [What LLM Forecasters Know but Don't Say: Probing Internal Representations for Calibration and Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08046)
*Raphaël Sarfati,Pratyush Ranjan Tiwari,Siddharth Boppana,Christopher J. Earls,Srikar Varadaraj,Eric Ho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，针对预测任务微调的大语言模型虽然准确但校准不良，且思维链推理可能不忠实反映证据。通过在中间激活上训练表征池化探针，可显著提升校准效果；探针还能作为“测谎器”检测推理不忠实行为，且预测阶段在推理前即已基本固定，利用此特性可节省30-47%生成token且不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在预测任务上表现准确但校准差，且思维链推理可能掩盖真实决策依据，需要更直接的方法来校准、审计和分流模型预测。

Method: 在Eternis-Forecaster 8B、GLM-4.7-Flash和GLM-4.5-Air上，利用OpenForesight数据集训练表征池化探针；通过证据消融和干扰注入评估思维链忠实度；采用强制回答实验分析预推理阶段的答案分布。

Result: 探针显著改善校准；探针激活比推理轨迹更能追踪行为变化，在84%案例中预测变化方向，甚至能在思维链掩盖扰动时检测到；单次预推理传递即可恢复承诺答案和置信度，基于答案分布分散度路由问题可节省30-47% token且不降准确率。

Conclusion: 探测内部表征是校准、审计和分流语言模型预测器及推理模型的实用工具。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned for forecasting can be accurate yet poorly calibrated, and their chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning may not faithfully reflect the evidence behind a forecast. We ask whether internal representations offer a more direct window into both. Working with Eternis-Forecaster 8B on OpenForesight, we train representation-pooling probes on intermediate activations and find they achieve substantially better calibration; a result that also holds for GLM-4.7-Flash and GLM-4.5-Air. We then assess CoT faithfulness through evidence ablation and diversionary injection: removing an influential source in the prompt often changes the model's forecast while leaving the reasoning trace untouched. The same probes function as lie detectors: their activations track behavioral shifts far better than the reasoning trace does, and they also predict the direction of change in 84% of cases, including when the CoT conceals the perturbation's influence. Finally, forced answering reveals that forecasts are largely fixed before reasoning begins: a single pre-reasoning pass recovers the committed answer and confidence, and routing questions by the spread of this pre-set answer distribution saves 30-47% of generated tokens, with no loss of accuracy. Together, these results establish probing internal representations as a practical tool for calibrating, auditing, and triaging language model forecasters and reasoning models more broadly.

</details>

### [44] [BACH: A Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads for Multi-Interest Two-Tower Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08107)
*Quoc Phong Nguyen,Paul Albert,Long Vuong,Vuong Le,Julien Monteil*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BACH 将多兴趣双塔检索建模为用户级别的混合模型，通过变分推断训练，缓解路由崩溃，提供每用户兴趣权重，并在三个大规模基准上超越硬路由和单向量基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双塔检索器仅用单一向量表示用户，难以捕捉多样化兴趣；多兴趣模型虽引入多头但硬路由训练导致头利用不足（路由崩溃），且无法给出每用户各兴趣的重要性估计。

Method: 提出 BACH（Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads），将多兴趣双塔检索建模为用户级混合模型，利用变分推断拟合。软混合机制使每个头都能被训练（缓解崩溃），输出每用户的兴趣权重供服务复用，并支持共享全局码本变体以实现预计算检索。

Result: 在 MovieLens-20M、Taobao、Netflix 三大基准上，BACH 在各头数量下均显著提升顶部检索效果，优于硬路由多兴趣和单向量基线。此外发现以服务一致的方式为每个候选打分（取最佳头）优于常规目标路由训练，BACH 进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: BACH 通过贝叶斯混合建模解决了多兴趣检索中的路由崩溃和兴趣重要性估计缺失问题，实现了更有效的多兴趣表示和服务时的个性化加权，在大规模推荐场景中表现优异。

Abstract: Two-tower retrievers compress each user into a single embedding, limiting their ability to serve diverse interests. Multi-interest models give each user several heads scored by a maximum inner product, but their hard-routing training under-utilizes heads (routing collapse) and gives no per-user estimate of how much each interest matters for serving. We present \textbf{BACH} (\emph{Bayesian Admixture of Contrastive Heads}), which casts multi-interest two-tower retrieval as a per-user mixture over the heads, fit by variational inference. The soft mixture trains every head (mitigating collapse), produces a per-user weighting of the interests that is reused at serving, and admits a shared global-codebook variant with precomputable retrieval. On three large-scale benchmarks, MovieLens-20M, Taobao, and Netflix, BACH improves top-of-ranking retrieval over hard-routing multi-interest and single-vector baselines at every head count; we further find that scoring every candidate by its best head, consistent with serving, outperforms the usual target-routed training, and that BACH improves further still.

</details>


### [45] [DaV-Gen: End-to-End Generative Retrieval via Draft-and-Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08365)
*Meng Zhao,Chunmei Liu,Qinyong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DaV-Gen，一种基于“草拟-验证”机制的统一生成式检索框架，旨在解决多级级联架构中目标不一致导致的误差传播，以及端到端生成模型自回归推理慢的问题。通过对比损失和融合损失联合训练，模型在单一模型内同时具备高效向量草拟和精准融合验证能力，推理时先快速草拟候选集再精细验证，实现了速度与精度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界主流检索系统采用多级级联架构（MCA），各阶段优化目标不一致导致早期误差传播放大，降低最终结果质量；端到端生成式模型虽能统一流程，但自回归解码严重制约在线服务性能。需要一种统一架构，兼具级联系统的效率和生成模型的精度。

Method: 提出 DaV-Gen 框架，借鉴推测解码思想，将生成任务重构为草拟和验证两个协同操作。训练阶段采用复合损失函数：1) 对比损失构建嵌入空间以支持高效草拟；2) 融合损失结合生成似然与向量相似度产生更优验证分数。推理阶段先进行向量化草拟生成候选集，再用融合评分函数验证候选。

Result: DaV-Gen 在统一的端到端架构中实现了稀疏草拟的速度与先进生成模型的精度，有效弥合了效率与效果之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: DaV-Gen 通过草拟-验证机制和联合训练策略，成功将推测解码思想引入工业级检索推荐，提供了一种兼顾高效检索与精准排序的新范式，为统一生成式信息检索系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Mainstream industrial information retrieval systems (e.g., search and recommendation) are usually built upon Multi-Stage Cascade Architectures (MCAs), which balance effectiveness and efficiency through a coarse-to-fine ``retrieval-ranking'' pipeline. However, the optimization objectives across different stages are substantially inconsistent, propagating or even amplifying the early-stage errors that ultimately degrade the quality of final results. While emerging end-to-end generative models offer a potential solution by unifying the pipeline, their online serving performance is severely hindered by the auto-regressive process inherited from the standard decoder-only structure. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{DaV-Gen}, a novel unified solution designed to fundamentally refactor the paradigm for both search and recommendation via a ``Draft-and-Verify'' mechanism. Inspired by the process used by speculative decoding, our framework redesigns the generation task into two synergistic operations within a single model. During training, the model is concurrently optimized for both candidate drafting and fine-grained verification. This is achieved by a composite loss function that jointly trains the model on two distinct but related objectives: 1) a contrastive loss that structures the embedding space for efficient drafting, and 2) a fusion loss that combines generative likelihood with vector similarity to produce a superior verification score. This integrated training strategy equips the model with dual capabilities. At inference time, it first performs highly efficient vector-based drafting to generate a candidate set, and then verifies these candidates using the more powerful fused scoring function, thereby achieving both the speed of sparse drafting and the precision of advanced generative models within a unified, end-to-end architecture.

</details>


### [46] [H3D: Benchmarking Unsupervised Text Hashing for Fine-Grained Document Deduplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08382)
*Qianren Mao,Jiaxun Lyu,Junnan Liu,Zhijun Chen,Jingzheng Li,Hanwen Hao,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: H3D是一个无监督文本哈希基准，用于细粒度文档去重，评估了多种非学习哈希方法（MinHash、SimHash、Winnowing、FuzzyHash、FlyHash）和基于冻结BGE嵌入的语义敏感方法（BGE-BIHash、BGE-LSHash），在CSFCube和RELISH数据集上展示了词法/结构指纹与语义敏感表示在近重复匹配和内容重写下的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究很少在统一协议下比较细粒度科学文档的哈希流水线，缺乏对无监督哈希方法在文档去重和相似性搜索中表现的系统性基准评估。

Method: 构建H3D基准，评估代表性无监督非学习哈希方法（基于手工设计的数学规则生成哈希指纹，无需训练或标签）和两种基于冻结BGE嵌入的语义敏感量化策略，在CSFCube（侧面级科学文档相似性）和RELISH（分割级生物医学相似性搜索）两个互补数据集上，联合报告排序质量（MAP、NDCG@20）、计算效率及受控文本压缩下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验揭示一致的性能权衡：词法和结构指纹在近重复匹配任务上表现有竞争力，而语义敏感表示在内容重写场景下更好地保留相似性，但计算开销更大。进一步分析了不同相似性度量在特定哈希表示下何时变得秩序等价，提升了方法比较的可解释性和可复现性。

Conclusion: H3D为无监督文本哈希提供了统一评估协议，明确了词法/结构方法与语义敏感方法的适用边界，并通过秩序等价性分析增强了基准的可解释性，为细粒度文档去重的方法选择提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Document hashing provides compact representations for efficient similarity search and document deduplication, but existing studies rarely compare hashing pipelines under a unified protocol for fine-grained scientific documents. H3D is an unsupervised text hashing benchmark for fine-grained document deduplication. It evaluates representative unsupervised non-learning hashing approaches (MinHash, SimHash, Winnowing, FuzzyHash, FlyHash) together with semantic-sensitive methods built from frozen BGE embeddings and two quantization strategies (BGE-BIHash and BGE-LSHash). The non-learning methods generate hash fingerprints through manually designed mathematical rules without training or labeled similarity pairs, which distinguishes them from neural semantic hashing models. We benchmark all methods on CSFCube and RELISH, two datasets that provide complementary evaluation settings: facet-level analysis for scientific-document similarity and larger-scale split-level evaluation for biomedical similarity search. H3D jointly reports ranking quality (MAP, NDCG@20), efficiency, and robustness under controlled text compression. The results show a consistent trade-off: lexical and structural fingerprints are competitive for near-duplicate matching, while semantic-sensitive representations better preserve similarity under content rewriting, at higher computational cost. We further analyze when different similarity measures become rank-equivalent for specific hash representations, improving the interpretability and reproducibility of method comparisons.

</details>

*Paula Font Solà,Adrià Molina Rodríguez,Josep Lladós*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向历史数字图书馆的文档分析系统，支持动态知识建模，利用图结构存储专家事实和检索事实，使语言模型能访问长期记忆，从而实现跨文档的复杂查询、链接发现及专家知识整合，生成更丰富全面的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的对话式数字图书馆系统虽擅长单文档抽取任务，但缺乏整体解读文档集合和动态融合专家知识的能力，难以处理跨文档长期依赖和隐性知识整合。

Method: 构建基于图的知识存储结构，存储专家档案员提供的事实及文档检索衍生的事实；系统通过持续专业交互，利用图索引作为语言模型的外部记忆，同时检索原始文档和已建模知识，支持动态知识建模与推理。

Result: 系统能处理涉及长期依赖的复杂查询，实现链接发现，整合原始资料中未显式存在的专家知识，从而生成更丰富、更全面的信息输出。

Conclusion: 该图增强的动态知识建模方法有效克服了传统RAG在整体语境理解和专家知识融合上的局限，为历史数字图书馆的深度语义访问提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Recent developments in digital libraries increasingly favor conversational and natural language access to information through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Although these approaches are effective for extractive tasks grounded in individual records, they remain limited in their ability to interpret document collections holistically and to incorporate expert knowledge dynamically. In this article, we present a document analysis system designed for the management of historical digital libraries that supports on-the-fly knowledge modeling. The system is equipped with the capability to store facts produced either by expert archivists or derived from document retrieval processes within a graph-based structure. Through continuous professional interaction, the system can retrieve information not only from primary sources such as documents, but also from previously modeled knowledge, with the graph-based index acting as a memory for the language model to access. This enables increasingly complex queries involving long-term dependencies across documents, link discovery, and the integration of expert knowledge that may not be explicitly present in the original sources. As a result, the proposed approach facilitates the generation of richer and more comprehensive information.

</details>


### [48] [Log-Insight: Automating Microservice Incident Diagnosis via Neuro-Symbolic Log Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08529)
*Carlos Garcia-Hernandez,Aymane Abdali,Guangyu Wu,Mingxue Wang,Fei Shen,Zhaoyu Pang,Yanbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出Log-Insight系统，通过六阶段流水线将海量日志压缩1,000-7,000倍并保留故障信号，结合LLM生成根因假设，在11个生产事故（110次运行）中实现MRR=0.790、Top-3准确率超90%、延迟<1分钟，法医证据部分显著提升运维采纳度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模微服务系统事故诊断时间紧迫，单次事故日志超200万行、约12亿字符，远超LLM上下文窗口；现有模板解析缺乏语义推理、深度学习检测输出黑盒信号、LLM流水线面临上下文溢出与领域幻觉，亟需一种能复刻SRE结构化调查流程的自动化系统。

Method: Log-Insight采用六阶段符号化流水线：采样、模式理解、模式聚类、统计异常排序等阶段复刻SRE手工分流工作流，将百万级原始事件压缩为紧凑证据档案（保留统计显著故障信号），再交由LLM综合生成假设报告。

Result: 在11个历史生产事故（110次运行，SRE验证真值）评估中：MRR=0.790，正确根因在Top-3假设中命中率超90%，端到端延迟<1分钟；系统化分析失效模式与缓解措施；法医证据部分（精确日志模板与偏斜统计）被运维一致认为是关键采纳因素，使系统角色从不透明神谕转变为调查助手。

Conclusion: Log-Insight通过符号化预处理与LLM推理协同，有效解决大规模日志根因分析的上下文溢出与幻觉问题，实现高准确率、低延迟的自动化事故诊断，且提供可解释的法医证据，显著提升生产环境可采纳性，为后续研究指明了失效模式缓解与开放方向。

Abstract: Diagnosing production incidents in large-scale microservice systems is time-critical for Site Reliability Engineers (SREs). A single 30-minute incident window in our deployment can generate over two million log lines--approximately 1.2 billion characters, far exceeding standard LLM context windows--making direct LLM-based Root Cause Analysis (RCA) infeasible. Existing approaches leave gaps: template-based parsers lack semantic anomaly reasoning, deep-learning detectors emit black-box binary signals, and LLM pipelines suffer context overflow and domain hallucination on raw telemetry.
  We present Log-Insight, an automated incident-diagnosis system deployed in production at Huawei. The core design principle automates the SRE's manual triage workflow: symbolic stages replicate the structured investigation a skilled SRE would perform--sampling, schema understanding, pattern clustering, and statistical anomaly ranking. This hands the LLM a compact, pre-ranked evidence dossier to synthesise into a hypothesis report. Our six-stage pipeline reduces millions of raw events by 1,000-7,000x while preserving statistically significant failure signals.
  Evaluated on 11 historical production incidents (110 runs, SRE-validated ground truth), Log-Insight achieves MRR = 0.790, returning the correct root cause within the top-3 hypotheses in over 90% of runs in under a minute of latency. We report systematic failure modes, active mitigations, and open research directions. The Forensic Evidence section--listing exact log templates and skew statistics--was consistently identified by operators as a key adoption factor, shifting the system's perceived role from opaque oracle to investigative assistant.

</details>


### [49] [Improving Ad-hoc Search Effectiveness for Conversational Information Retrieval via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08540)
*Ahmed Rayane Kebir,Jose G. Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出模型融合作为一种无需训练的策略，将临时检索模型和对话检索模型融合为单一模型，无需额外微调即可在两种设置下工作，实验显示融合显著提升临时检索能力并改善泛化性，在零样本条件下 NDCG@3 提升高达 15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话信息检索面临话题转移和共指消解挑战，现有方法依赖在对话数据集上微调临时检索器或多任务学习，但这些方法成本高、需要重新训练，且会导致灾难性遗忘，丧失基础临时检索性能。

Method: 引入模型融合作为免训练策略，采用线性和非线性参数级融合策略（Model Soup 和 Slerp），在标准临时搜索和对话检索数据集上进行实验。

Result: 模型融合显著增强对话检索器的临时搜索能力，同时提升跨任务特定数据集的泛化性，在零样本条件下 NDCG@3 最高提升 15%。

Conclusion: 模型融合是一种有效的免训练方法，能够将临时检索和对话检索能力结合在单一模型中，避免灾难性遗忘，降低训练成本，并提升零样本泛化性能。

Abstract: Conversational information retrieval is challenging since it requires the consideration of the conversation history which potentially gives rise to topic shifts and coreference resolution across previous turns. To address these challenges, previous work mainly rely on traditional fine-tuning of ad-hoc retrievers on conversational datasets or extrapolates their generalizability through multi-tasking. However, this mainstream approach is costly - since it requires model re-training - and exhibits catastrophic forgetting, where the model loses its foundational ad-hoc retrieval performance. In this paper, we fill this gap by introducing model merging as a training-free strategy enabling the design of a single retrieval model that operates across both ad-hoc and conversational settings with no additional fine-tuning. We conduct experiments using linear and non-linear parameter-wise merging strategies - namely Model Soup and Slerp - on standard ad-hoc search and conversational retrieval datasets. Our results demonstrate that model merging significantly enhances the ad-hoc search capabilities of conversational retrievers while improving generalizability across task-specific datasets, achieving up to 15% higher NDCG@3 under zero-shot conditions.

</details>

### [50] [Towards the Explainability of Temporal Graph Networks via Memory Backtracking and Topological Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07716)
*Yazheng Liu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MemExplainer，通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树结合LRP，实现对时序图网络预测的忠实解释，并在九个数据集上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释方法忽略了TGN的核心记忆模块，无法探究历史事件对预测的影响，需填补这一空白。

Method: 构建拓扑归因树捕获邻居及其记忆向量的影响，构建记忆回溯树量化历史事件如何塑造节点记忆；应用LRP保证事件贡献总和等于logits；设计优化目标解决top-k选择因非线性映射导致的不忠实问题。

Result: 在九个时序图数据集上（节点属性预测、链接预测、图分类任务）实验表明，该方法提供忠实解释并优于最先进基线。

Conclusion: MemExplainer通过显式建模记忆模块的历史贡献，实现了对TGN预测的忠实可解释性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Temporal graphs are ubiquitous in real-world applications and Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) have achieved superior predictive accuracy. Understanding which historical events drive model predictions can enhance trustworthiness of TGNs. Existing explanation methods overlook the memory module, the core component that records and updates node histories, leaving the influence of past events unexplored. To address this, we attribute TGNs predictions through the topology attribution tree and memory backtracking tree. The topology attribution tree captures the influence of neighbors and their memory vectors, then the memory backtracking tree quantifies how historical events shape node memory vectors. We apply the LRP in TGNs, ensuring that the total contribution of events equals the logits of model. Finally, top-k selection may be unfaithful due to the nonlinear mapping from logits to probabilities, we design optimization objectives to identify the important events. Experiments on nine temporal graph datasets, spanning node property prediction, link prediction tasks and graph classification tasks, show that our method provides faithful explanations and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yazhengliu/MemExplainer

</details>


### [51] [Who Gets Missed in the Tail? Thresholded Subgroup Underdiagnosis in Long-Tailed Chest X-ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07717)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Min Xu,Trung-Nghia Le,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨胸部X光多标签分类中长尾类别与子群体的公平性问题，提出诊断阶梯方法结合组尾加权与尾感知阈值选择，显著降低尾部假阴性率与最差子群假阴性率，并指出公平性取决于发现、子群体与操作阈值的联合作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光分类中，即便排序性能可接受，长尾罕见阳性患者仍可能因阈值设定被漏诊，尤其在子群体中更显著。研究旨在审计部署前模型从得分转决策时谁被遗漏，解决长尾多标签场景下的子群体公平性问题。

Method: 使用诊断阶梯分离类别级长尾损失、子群体感知加权、群体鲁棒性与阈值选择；在VinDr-CXR与MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT数据集上，采用组尾加权结合尾感知阈值调整，对比GroupDRO基线，通过配对bootstrap验证阈值化FNR降低的显著性。

Result: VinDr-CXR上，尾部FNR从0.665降至0.269，性别最差组FNR从0.705降至0.157，年龄最差组FNR从0.822降至0.133，macro-mAP从0.611升至0.635；MIMIC-CXR上尾部FNR从0.866降至0.741，各子群最差FNR均下降，但残余漏诊率仍高。GroupDRO仅靠群体鲁棒性无法消除罕见子群漏诊。

Conclusion: 胸部X光罕见标签公平性取决于具体发现、子群体与操作阈值的联合作用，而非仅靠标签频率或排序指标；需在部署前针对子群体与阈值进行联合审计与调整。

Abstract: In chest X-ray (CXR) classification, acceptable ranking performance can still leave rare-positive patients below threshold, especially within subgroups. We study this pre-deployment fairness problem as an audit question: after a long-tailed multi-label CXR model is converted from scores into decisions, who is missed? Across VinDr-CXR and MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, we use a diagnostic ladder to separate class-level long-tail losses, subgroup-aware weighting, group robustness, and threshold selection. On VinDr-CXR, group-tail weighting followed by tail-aware thresholding reduces tail FNR from 0.665 to 0.269, sex worst-group FNR from 0.705 to 0.157, and age worst-group FNR from 0.822 to 0.133, while macro-mAP increases from 0.611 to 0.635. On MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, the same score-to-threshold comparison reduces tail FNR from 0.866 to 0.741 and lowers worst-group FNR across sex, age, race, and insurance; residual missed-positive rates nevertheless remain high. Paired bootstrap contrasts on VinDr support the thresholded FNR reductions, and GroupDRO reference runs indicate that aggregate group robustness alone does not remove rare subgroup misses in this setting. The study supports a narrow audit claim: rare-label fairness in CXR depends jointly on the finding, subgroup, and operating threshold, not on label frequency or ranking metrics alone.

</details>


### [52] [LLT: Local Linear Transformer for PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07718)
*Oded Ovadia,Eli Turkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Local Linear Transformer (LLT)用于PDE算子学习，结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合，并融入坐标和几何信息，在多种PDE问题和离散化类型上实现了高精度且计算效率高的算子学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子虽然能学习长程依赖，但标准注意力机制存在两大局限：随计算节点数二次方缩放，且缺乏对局部交互的显式偏置。需要一种既能处理长程依赖又能高效捕捉局部交互、且适应多种离散化网格的PDE算子学习方法。

Method: 提出Local Linear Transformer (LLT)架构，结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合，并显式融入坐标和几何信息。该方法在弹性、塑性、机翼流动、管道流动和Darcy流动等多个PDE问题上评估，涵盖有限元、有限体积、有限差分等离散化方式，以及结构化和非结构化网格。

Result: 与先前研究的神经算子和Transformer基线相比，LLT在各问题上达到竞争性或更低的相对L2误差。在匹配的结构化离散化上，每训练迭代的墙钟时间比Transolver降低1.8-2.5倍。方法还成功扩展到包含32,186个非结构化网格点的三维汽车空气动力学数据集。

Conclusion: LLT为跨离散化、网格类型和问题设置的PDE问题提供了一个精确且计算高效的算子学习框架，有效解决了标准注意力的二次缩放和缺乏局部偏置的问题。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a common approach for learning PDE solution maps and accelerating numerical simulations. Transformer-based neural operators are of particular interest, since attention can learn long-range dependencies in the computational domain. However, standard attention has two major limitations when applied to PDEs: it scales quadratically with the number of computational nodes, and it lacks an explicit bias toward local interactions. To address these issues, we introduce Local Linear Transformer (LLT) for PDE operator learning. The architecture combines linear global attention with local spatial mixing, and incorporates coordinate and geometry information. We evaluate LLT on several PDE problems, including elasticity, plasticity, airfoil flow, pipe flow, and Darcy flow. The reference data for these problems span finite-element, finite-volume, and finite-difference discretizations on structured and unstructured meshes. Compared with other neural-operator and transformer baselines from prior studies, LLT achieves competitive or lower relative $L_2$ error across these problems. On matched structured discretizations, wall-clock time per training iteration is reduced by factors of 1.8 to 2.5 relative to Transolver. We also scale the approach and apply it to a three-dimensional car aerodynamics dataset with 32,186 unstructured mesh points per sample. Together, these results indicate that LLT provides an accurate and computationally efficient operator for PDE problems across discretizations, mesh types, and problem settings.

</details>


### [53] [ReCoLoRA: Spectrum-Aware Recursive Consolidation for Continual LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07719)
*Wentao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoLoRA 提出了一种递归合并低秩适配器的方法，通过频谱感知的初始化、肘部准则选择有效秩、主子空间优先适配以及递归重新分解当前有效权重，解决了 LoRA 类方法在持续微调中因堆叠低秩更新导致的灾难性遗忘问题。在四个 7-8B 模型上的六任务持续 GLUE 基准中，ReCoLoRA 在三个骨干网络上取得最佳最终平均分，且训练参数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法（如 LoRA）在单任务上表现良好，但在任务序列中会将低秩更新堆叠在同一冻结权重上，导致新任务覆盖旧任务知识，即灾难性遗忘。需要一种能够累积吸收前序任务知识、同时保持参数效率的持续微调框架。

Method: ReCoLoRA 采用频谱感知策略：1) 从预训练权重的随机 SVD 初始化适配器；2) 使用肘部准则按层选择有效秩；3) 先适配主子空间再开放残差容量；4) 每个新任务前，对当前有效权重（而非原始权重）进行递归重新分解，得到冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和全新适配器（递归合并），使每个任务都从已吸收前序任务的模型开始。

Result: 在四个 7-8B 骨干模型上的六任务持续 GLUE 序列实验中，ReCoLoRA 在三个骨干上对比 rank-swept LoRA、PiSSA、AdaLoRA、DoRA 等基线取得最佳最终平均分，且训练参数更少；基于 oracle 路由的任务库变体作为完全任务隔离的上界。

Conclusion: ReCoLoRA 通过递归合并低秩适配器，有效缓解了持续微调中的灾难性遗忘，在保持参数效率的前提下实现了优于现有方法的持续学习性能。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts a large language model to one task cheaply, but across a task sequence LoRA-style methods keep stacking low-rank updates on the same frozen weight, so each new task tends to overwrite the previous ones. We present ReCoLoRA (Recursive Consolidation of Low-Rank Adapters), a spectrum-aware framework for continual fine-tuning: adapters are initialized from a randomized SVD of the pretrained weight, per-layer effective ranks are selected by an elbow criterion, and the principal subspace is adapted before residual capacity is opened. Before each new task, ReCoLoRA re-decomposes the current effective weight, rather than the original one, into a frozen residual, a slowly updated principal component, and a fresh adapter (recursive consolidation), so every task starts from the model that has already absorbed its predecessors. On a six-task continual GLUE sequence over four 7-8B backbones, ReCoLoRA attains the best final average score on three of the four backbones against rank-swept LoRA, PiSSA, AdaLoRA, and DoRA baselines while training fewer parameters; an oracle-routed task-bank variant serves as an upper bound under full task isolation. Code: https://github.com/bhqy666/ReCoLoRA.

</details>


### [54] [Omni-Sleep: A Sleep Foundation Model via Hierarchical Contrastive Learning of CNS--ANS Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07720)
*Zhoujie Hou,Song Wang,Kexin Lou,Mo Wang,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Omni-Sleep，一种利用中枢神经系统（CNS）与自主神经系统（ANS）生理拓扑先验的睡眠基础模型，通过拓扑约束的表征学习在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上超越现有基线，展现更强的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和缺失模态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型在融合异构生物信号（EEG、EOG、EMG、ECG、呼吸等）时忽略了其生理组织结构，采用拓扑无关的融合方式，导致难以捕捉大脑-身体协同动力学，限制了表征的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 Omni-Sleep，引入 CNS/ANS 分区作为生理先验，设计三大目标：1) 系统内一致性，捕捉神经与心肺子系统内部的共享因子；2) 系统间同步，对齐子系统轨迹以建模脑-体动力学；3) 潜在空间掩码时序建模，捕捉长程睡眠动态。在超 10 万小时多中心多模态 PSG 数据上预训练。

Result: 在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务中，跨数据集和模态消融设置下，Omni-Sleep 均优于强基础模型基线，显著提升标签效率、跨数据集泛化性能及缺失模态鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 生理层级先验（CNS/ANS 拓扑）对于可泛化的睡眠表征学习至关重要，拓扑约束的多目标学习能有效整合多模态生理信号，为睡眠医学 AI 提供了更鲁棒的基础模型范式。

Abstract: Sleep physiology arises from the coordinated dynamics of the central nervous system (CNS) and autonomic nervous system (ANS), as reflected by multimodal polysomnography signals including EEG, EOG, EMG, ECG, and respiration. However, existing sleep foundation models often fuse heterogeneous biosignals in a topology-agnostic manner, overlooking their physiological organization. We introduce Omni-Sleep, a sleep foundation model that uses the CNS/ANS partition as a physiological prior for topology-constrained representation learning. Omni-Sleep learns structured representations through three objectives: intra-system consistency, which captures shared subsystem-level factors within neural and cardio-respiratory signals; inter-system synchronization, which aligns subsystem trajectories to model brain--body dynamics; and latent-space masked temporal modeling, which captures long-horizon sleep dynamics. Pre-trained on over 100,000 hours of multi-center multimodal PSG data, Omni-Sleep is evaluated on sleep staging and multi-disease classification. Across datasets and modality-ablation settings, Omni-Sleep outperforms strong foundation-model baselines, showing improved label efficiency, cross-dataset generalization, and robustness to missing modalities. These results highlight the value of physiological hierarchy for generalizable sleep representation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/AutoBrain-sleep/OmniSleep.

</details>


### [55] [Uncertainty-gated selection for block-sparse attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07724)
*Thomas Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息价值的路由机制，用于改进块稀疏注意力中的top-k选择，通过测量每个查询的top-k切割决断程度，在得分差距最小的查询上扩大保留集合，显著提升长上下文语言模型的召回率和准确性，同时保持较低的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块稀疏注意力通过top-k选择替代O(N^2) softmax来扩展长上下文语言模型，但这种硬性截断是短视的：当第k个和第k+1个块的得分接近时，选择器在不增加预算的情况下做出决定，导致携带答案证据的块被丢弃且无法在后续层恢复。

Method: 提出一个信息价值路由器，为每个查询测量top-k切割的决断程度（即第k个和第k+1个块的得分差距），并将得分差距最小的查询的保留集合加倍。该规则与骨干网络无关，可与现有的块评分方法（如Quest）堆叠使用。

Result: 在LongBench-v2 medium（n=215）上，router-on-Quest的成对召回率达到0.75，优于top-k的0.47（+28个百分点，McNemar p<0.01），在RULER NIAH多键任务上距离稠密注意力仅2个百分点。该提升在四个模型、三种架构（Qwen2.5、Mistral-Nemo、Qwen3.6）上复现。在128K上下文下，路由器在Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3.6上分别保留了稠密注意力0.81和0.89的准确性（而SSA风格top-k在前者上仅0.09），融合选择加内核流水线的运行时间分别为稠密注意力的0.62倍和0.80倍。

Conclusion: 信息价值路由器有效缓解了块稀疏注意力中top-k选择的短视性，以极小的计算开销显著提升了长上下文任务的性能，接近稠密注意力的效果，且具有模型无关性和易于集成的优势。

Abstract: Block-sparse attention scales long-context language models by replacing the O(N^2) softmax with a per-query top-k selection over key blocks. This cutoff is myopic: when the k-th and (k+1)-th blocks are nearly tied in score, the selector commits without spending extra budget, and a dropped block carrying answer evidence is unrecoverable downstream. We propose a value-of-information router that measures, for each query, how decisively the top-k cut was made, and doubles the kept set for the queries where that gap is smallest; the rule is backbone-agnostic and stacks with existing block-scoring methods such as Quest. On LongBench-v2 medium at n=215 (the entire dataset subset), router-on-Quest reaches paired recall 0.75 vs. top-k 0.47 -- +28 pp over the SSA-style baseline (McNemar p<0.01) -- and lands within 2 pp of dense on RULER NIAH multikey at the same context. The lift reproduces on four models from three architectures (Qwen2.5, Mistral-Nemo, Qwen3.6). At 128K, the router preserves 0.81 and 0.89 of dense accuracy on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3.6 (vs. SSA-style top-k at 0.09 on the former) while the fused selection-plus-kernel pipeline runs at 0.62x and 0.80x dense wall time.

</details>


### [56] [SHIFT: Survival Prediction from Incomplete and Heterogeneous Genomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07725)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Ayhan Can Erdur,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Benedikt Wiestler,Jana Lipkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SHIFT模型，利用Transformer的掩码自注意力机制和特征可用性掩码，直接从不完整的基因组数据进行生存预测，无需测试时填补，解决了多中心基因组面板差异导致的结构性特征缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨机构基因组预测模型常因测序面板不同导致结构性特征缺失而失效，现有方法（限制共同基因、排除不完整患者、测试时填补）会降低鲁棒性并限制多中心数据利用。

Method: SHIFT将每个基因组特征单独表示，采用掩码自注意力和特征可用性掩码，使预测仅基于观测输入；训练时引入变速率特征掩码以增强对异质缺失模式的鲁棒性。

Result: 在胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞癌多队列外部验证中（含严重面板错配场景），SHIFT泛化能力强，优于标准生存基线和填补方法，单一模型适应不同特征集；纳入不完整队列患者训练可提升外部数据性能。

Conclusion: 缺失感知建模是精准肿瘤学多中心生存预测的实用策略，部分观测队列无需从模型构建中排除。

Abstract: Genomic prediction models often fail to transfer across institutions because sequencing panels differ across sites, creating structural feature missingness at deployment. Existing approaches to this challenge typically restrict analysis to genes shared across cohorts, exclude patients with incomplete profiles, or rely on test-time imputation, all of which can reduce robustness and limit the use of multi-center data. We propose Survival prediction Handling Incomplete Features using Transformer (SHIFT), a missingness-aware survival model that directly predicts from incomplete genomic inputs without test-time imputation. SHIFT represents each genomic feature separately and uses masked self-attention, along with a feature-availability mask, so that predictions are based only on observed inputs. Further, we introduce variable-rate feature masking during training to improve robustness to heterogeneous missingness patterns. We evaluate the approach on glioblastoma and lung squamous cell carcinoma with external validation across multiple cohorts, including a challenging setting with severe cross-cohort panel mismatch. Across these settings, SHIFT shows strong generalization and compares favorably with standard survival baselines and imputation-based approaches, while using a single model across differing feature sets. We also find that incorporating patients from incomplete cohorts during development can improve performance on external data, suggesting that partially observed cohorts need not be excluded from model building. These results support missingness-aware modeling as a practical strategy for multi-center survival prediction in precision oncology.

</details>


### [57] [Jet-Long: Efficient Long-Context Extension with Dynamic Bifocal RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07740)
*Haozhan Tang,Zerui Wang,Yuxian Gu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jet-Long 是一种无需训练的零样本长上下文扩展方法，采用双焦点注意力机制：局部窗口保持 RoPE 保真，长距离窗口动态调整缩放因子，在短上下文恢复基础模型性能、长上下文实现干净外推，并通过融合 CuTe 内核实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本长上下文扩展方法通常固定单一缩放因子，激进因子损害短上下文保真度，保守因子在长上下文失效。随着 LLMs 部署于 RAG、仓库级编码、智能体工作流等长上下文场景，输入常超预训练窗口一个数量级，亟需一种既能在短上下文保持基础模型性能、又能在长上下文稳健外推、且推理开销极低的方法。

Method: 提出 Jet-Long：1) 双焦点注意力构造——局部窗口使用原始 RoPE 保持保真度，长距离窗口采用随序列长度动态调整的缩放因子；2) 包含-排除注意力合并机制，无缝融合两个窗口的注意力输出；3) 即时 RoPE 修正旋转，消除长距离窗口的位置编码偏移；4) 将上述操作融合进单个 CuTe 内核，实现预填阶段高达 1.39× FA2 吞吐、单批生成 ≤4% 开销。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 上测试至 128K 上下文：RULER 基准分别领先最强基线 +4.79/+2.18/+2.03 pp；在 HELMET-RAG（HELMET 认定的最高效下游长上下文预测基准）取得最佳整体准确率；PG-19 困惑度最低。泛化至混合注意力架构（如 Jet-Nemotron）可进一步提升长上下文性能，且超参数鲁棒性强，部署友好。

Conclusion: Jet-Long 通过动态双焦点注意力与高内核融合，解决了零样本长上下文扩展中短/长上下文性能的权衡难题，在保持基础模型短上下文保真度的同时实现长上下文稳健外推，且推理效率接近硬件极限，为开源权重模型的长上下文部署提供了通用、高效、免训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are increasingly deployed in long-context applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, repository-level coding, and agentic workflows whose accumulated reasoning and tool traces routinely push the input an order of magnitude past the pretraining window, making zero-shot context extension the dominant deployment path for open-weight checkpoints. Most existing zero-shot methods fix a single rescaling factor up front, so an aggressive factor sacrifices short-context fidelity while a conservative one breaks down at long contexts. We propose Jet-Long, a tuning-free zero-shot method that pairs a local RoPE-faithful window with a long-range window whose rescaling factor adapts dynamically to the current sequence length, recovering the base model exactly at short inputs while extrapolating cleanly at long ones. An inclusion-exclusion attention merge and an on-the-fly RoPE correction rotation make the bifocal construction essentially free at inference; fused into a single CuTe kernel, long-context prefill reaches up to $1.39\times$ FA2 throughput on H100 (approaching the Hopper-only FA4), and single-batch generation incurs $\le 4\%$ overhead at every length. On Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B up to 128K context, Jet-Long leads RULER by $+4.79$/$+2.18$/$+2.03$~pp over the strongest baseline at 1.7B/4B/8B, achieves the best overall accuracy on HELMET-RAG (a benchmark identified by HELMET as the most efficient predictor of downstream long-context performance) and attains the lowest PG-19 perplexity. Jet-Long also generalizes to hybrid attention architectures such as Jet-Nemotron for further long-context improvement without retraining, and remains hyperparameter-resilient for ease of deployment.

</details>


### [58] [Architecture Generalization with MetaNCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07743)
*Meet Barot,Daniel Berenberg,Sina Khajehabdollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA)，一种通过学习局部更新规则来自组织生成神经网络权重的框架。利用基于线性注意力的 Weight Transformer 作为规则网络，仅通过计算图上的局部交互迭代更新任务网络权重，无需反向传播即可生成多种架构（MLP、CNN、ResNet）的权重，并在 MNIST 和 CIFAR-100 上达到可扩展性能（高达 200 万参数），且泛化至未见架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物神经元通过局部突触交互实现高效学习和终身适应的启发，以及神经元胞状自动机 (NCA) 在形态发生中展现的稳定性和鲁棒性，作者旨在将这种基于局部规则的自组织能力迁移到人工神经网络权重的生成中，以实现无需反向传播、可泛化至多样架构的权重生成。

Method: 1. 构建 MetaNCA 框架：一个可学习的规则网络在计算图上仅通过局部交互迭代更新任务网络的权重。2. 提出 Weight Transformer 架构：采用线性注意力机制聚合邻近权重和隐藏状态的信息，作为规则网络的核心。3. 元训练阶段在多种架构（MLP、CNN、ResNet）上优化规则网络，使其能生成不同规模和结构的任务网络权重。

Result: MetaNCA 能为 MLP、CNN、ResNet 生成权重，在 MNIST 和 CIFAR-100 上达到具有竞争力的精度，扩展至 200 万参数规模。规则网络泛化至元训练未见的架构，且训练阶段的架构多样性显著增强了这种泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaNCA 证明了基于局部规则的自组织过程可以替代传统反向传播来生成神经网络权重，展示了跨架构泛化的潜力，为可扩展、适应性强的神经网络权重生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Self-organization is an emergent property of life, driven by the collective behavior of individual components acting on local information. Biological neurons, through local interactions transmitted through synapses, are able to learn efficiently and can adapt their connections over an organism's lifespan. Motivated by these desirable properties of adaptability and local interaction, neural cellular automata (NCA) models have been successful at learning morphogenesis solely through local update rules, demonstrating stability over many updates and robustness to perturbations. In this work, we introduce Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA), a framework that learns local rules which self-organize the weights of artificial neural networks. A learned rule network iteratively updates the weights of a task network using only local interactions on the computation graph. We propose a novel Weight Transformer architecture for the local rule network, which uses linear attention to aggregate signals from neighboring weights and hidden states. Once trained, the rule network generates task networks of diverse architectures without backpropagation. We show that MetaNCA generates weights for feedforward MLPs, CNNs, and ResNets on MNIST and CIFAR-100, scaling to networks of 2 million parameters. We further show that MetaNCA generalizes to architectures not seen during meta-training, and that architectural diversity in the training phase strengthens this generalization.

</details>


### [59] [LiST: Lipschitz Scaling Training for Robust and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07745)
*Arthur Chiron,Franck Mamalet,Thomas Massena,Thomas Deltort,Mathieu Serrurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为 LiST（Lipschitz Scaling Training）的新训练范式，通过迭代调整全局 Lipschitz 常数，在准确率、鲁棒性和校准之间实现三方平衡，并基于校准自动寻找 Pareto 最优工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将准确率、鲁棒性和校准分别处理，Lipschitz 约束模型虽能保证鲁棒性，但手动选择约束 L 难以平衡准确率与鲁棒性，且其校准性质未被充分探索。论文旨在建立 Lipschitz 约束与 Temperature Scaling 的理论联系，利用校准作为自动选择 Pareto 前沿工作点的准则。

Method: 提出 LiST 训练范式：通过迭代调整全局 Lipschitz 常数 L 以达到校准最优的 L*，并引入损失函数中的 margin 参数构建全校准 Pareto 前沿；收敛后可将校准数据重新融入训练，提升样本效率且不损失校准性能。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10/100 和 Tiny-ImageNet 上验证，LiST 在准确率和鲁棒性上均与受约束及无约束基线具有竞争力，且无需额外校准步骤即可实现开箱即用的校准。

Conclusion: LiST 通过将校准作为选择 Lipschitz 约束的原则性标准，统一了准确率、鲁棒性和校准三大目标，提供了一种可自动导航准确率-鲁棒性权衡并保持校准的实用训练框架。

Abstract: While accuracy, robustness, and calibration are all essential for reliable neural networks, they are often studied separately; developing models that satisfy all three simultaneously remains a central challenge. Lipschitz-constrained models guarantee robustness by design, yet the manual selection of the Lipschitz constraint L governs the resulting accuracy-robustness trade-off, and their calibration properties remain largely underexplored. In this work, we highlight a theoretical and empirical link between the enforced Lipschitz constraint and Temperature Scaling, a state-of-the-art calibration method. Specifically, we find that for a given training scheme, there exists a non-trivial value L* that yields an out-of-the-box calibrated network, and that calibration acts as a principled criterion to select a well-defined operating point on the accuracy-robustness Pareto front. Leveraging these insights, we introduce Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST), a novel training paradigm that iteratively adjusts the global Lipschitz constant to reach this operating point. Through a margin parameter in the training loss, LiST further enables the construction of a fully calibrated Pareto front, allowing users to navigate the accuracy-robustness trade-off while remaining calibrated throughout. At convergence, LiST also enables the reintegration of calibration data into training, improving sample efficiency without sacrificing calibration. We validate LiST on CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet, demonstrating competitive accuracy and robustness against constrained and unconstrained baselines, while remaining calibrated out of the box. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [60] [Selective Left-Shift: Turning Test-Time Compute and Difficulty-based Curation into Training Data for Low-Resource Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07748)
*Didula Samaraweera,Anjana Supun,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三阶段流水线解决小语言模型在低资源编程语言上的代码生成三难困境：离线数据合成、SFT嵌入语法先验、基于可验证奖励的强化学习。在Qwen3-8B上显著提升Julia和Ballerina性能，且数据和成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高资源语言（如Python、Java）上代码生成强，但在低资源编程语言（LRPLs）如Julia上性能急剧下降。改进小语言模型面临三难困境：SFT受限于数据稀缺，推理时扩展部署成本太高，从头进行强化学习收益近乎为零。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1）左移推理计算至离线数据合成引擎，利用迭代编译器和测试反馈生成验证过的训练样本；2）在合成验证数据上微调SLM以嵌入强语法先验；3）应用基于语言无关输入输出测试的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），SFT先验约束探索避免语法错误。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，MultiPL-E pass@1提升高达+7.6点，Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia提升+14.2点，优于SOTA。仅使用1/3数据量和1/6成本。泛化到Ballerina达到49.7% MultiPL-E Pass@1（该语言预训练表示近乎零）。消融实验确认SFT阶段和执行落地奖励对稳定训练均必要。

Conclusion: 该流水线有效解决了低资源编程语言代码生成的三难困境，通过解耦语法习得与算法推理，以极低成本显著提升小语言模型在LRPLs上的性能，且泛化能力强。

Abstract: Large Language Models achieve strong code generation for high resource languages like Python and Java but suffer sharp performance drops on Low-Resource Programming Languages~(LRPLs) such as Julia. Improving Small Language Models~(SLMs) for these languages faces a trilemma: Supervised Fine-Tuning~(SFT) is bottlenecked by data scarcity, inference-time scaling is too expensive for deployment, and Reinforcement Learning from scratch yields near zero advantages. We propose a three-phase pipeline that resolves this trilemma by decoupling syntax acquisition from algorithmic reasoning. First, we \emph{left-shift} inference-time compute to an offline data synthesis engine that uses iterative compiler and test feedback to generate verified training examples. Second, we fine-tune an SLM on this synthetic, verified data to embed strong syntactic priors. Third, we apply Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward~(RLVR) grounded by language-agnostic Input/Output tests, where the SFT prior constrains exploration away from syntax errors. Applied to Qwen3-8B, our pipeline improves pass@1 by up to +7.6 points on MultiPL-E and +14.2 points on the Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia compared to SOTA results. Furthermore, the pipeline only used $\frac{1}{3}$ data and $\frac{1}{6}$ cost over the previous state-of-the-art. We further demonstrate that the pipeline generalizes to Ballerina achieving 49.7\% MultiPL-E Pass@1, a language with near-zero pretraining representation. Ablations confirm that both the SFT phase and execution-grounded rewards are necessary for stable training.

</details>


### [61] [A Transdiagnostic Space of Disorder Like Phenotypes in Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07753)
*Hari Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评价引导的PPO智能体框架，通过单一可控旋钮操纵认知评价信号来建模七种心理障碍（焦虑、躁狂、强迫检查、抑郁、冲动、成瘾、PTSD），实现了分级单调的剂量-反应关系，并发现障碍在二维情感空间中自我组织、治疗反应差异及共病非加性交互，且框架可迁移至三维像素环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过手工调整奖励塑造在强化学习智能体中诱导单一或少数障碍，事后标记行为且仅报告单次运行，缺乏系统性、可控性和统计严谨性。本文旨在将障碍建模重构为认知评价信号的可剂量控制操纵，建立计算精神病学账户与可测量症状检测的对应关系，提供可复现、可干预的情感表型参数空间。

Method: 采用评价引导的PPO智能体，将七种障碍各自建模为单一评价权重旋钮（基于计算精神病学理论），配合预注册的症状检测范式（映射至公认实验范式）。进行大规模实验：10个随机种子、4种控制条件、95%置信区间，累计超1000次运行。进一步在MiniWorld三维像素环境中使用标准卷积智能体（无评价评论家）验证三种障碍旋钮的迁移性。

Result: 1) 所有七种障碍均表现出分级单调的剂量-反应曲线，控制条件无法复现。2) 障碍在二维情感空间自我组织，躁狂与焦虑镜像对称。3) 移除旋钮可缓解奖励扭曲型障碍（躁狂、检查、成瘾），但回避型障碍（焦虑、PTSD）需分级暴露课程恢复。4) 双旋钮交互呈非加性，产生可检验的共病预测。5) 评价权重参数化了可控情感表型空间，同一旋钮可建模治疗。6) 抑郁、成瘾、焦虑三旋钮成功迁移至MiniWorld，跨领域交叉检测解离得到确认，证明框架不依赖网格世界或PPO评价评论家。

Conclusion: 评价权重旋钮提供了一个可控、可迁移的情感表型参数空间，统一了障碍诱导与治疗建模，揭示了障碍的内在结构组织（二维情感空间、治疗反应分类、共病非加性交互），为计算精神病学和情感控制失效模式研究提供了严谨、可扩展的计算平台。

Abstract: Modelling psychological disorders in artificial agents offers both a testbed for computational psychiatry and a lens on the failure modes of affective control. Prior work induces one or two disorders in a reinforcement learning (RL) agent by hand-tuned reward shaping, labels the behaviour post hoc, and reports single runs. We recast disorder modelling as dose-controllable manipulation of cognitive appraisal signals in an appraisal-guided PPO agent, expressing seven disorders (anxiety, mania, obsessive-compulsive checking, depression, impulsivity, addiction, and post-traumatic stress) each as a single knob grounded in a computational psychiatry account, with each symptom measured by a preregistered assay mapped to a recognised paradigm. Across more than a thousand runs (10 seeds, four controls, 95% confidence intervals) every disorder shows a graded, monotone dose-response that no control reproduces. Beyond these induced effects, three findings emerge that were not written into the reward: the disorders self-organise into a two-dimensional affective space in which mania mirrors anxiety; removing a knob remits reward distortion disorders (mania, checking, addiction) but not avoidance disorders (anxiety, PTSD), which instead recover under a graded exposure curriculum; and two simultaneous knobs interact nonadditively, yielding testable comorbidity predictions. Appraisal weights thus parameterise a controllable space of affective phenotypes in which the same knobs that induce a disorder can model its treatment. We also show that three disorder knobs (depression, addiction, anxiety) transfer to a three-dimensional pixel environment (MiniWorld) with a standard convolutional agent and no appraisal critic, with cross-assay dissociation confirmed across both domains, indicating the framework is not specific to grid worlds or to PPO's appraisal critic.

</details>


### [62] [Image classification via a quantum-inspired strategy involving a mixture of experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07754)
*Kumari Jyoti,Rohith Babu,Apoorva D. Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合经典-量子框架，利用量子启发的混合专家策略进行图像模式识别，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上表现优于单一专家，错误率降低约一半，且在GPU上开销适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典卷积神经网络依赖基于扩散的模糊化和分块池化进行下采样，作者旨在探索更高效的量子启发策略，结合混合专家机制提升特征提取与分类性能。

Method: 量子部分：振幅编码图像、局部酉操作卷积、多专家并行处理（参数不同）、量子稳定子码提取特征。经典部分：将各专家提取的特征联合送入全连接神经网络进行类别预测。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST基准上，联合专家分析优于单一专家，图像分类失败率降低约2倍；量子启发策略在GPU工作站上开销适中，具备实用性，且指出了量子部分在量子处理器上执行的路径。

Conclusion: 该混合经典-量子混合专家框架为图像模式识别提供了一种高效且实用的替代方案，展示了量子启发方法在经典硬件上的优势，并为未来在真实量子设备上部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: Pattern recognition problems arise in a variety of physical image processing situations, and convolutional neural networks are a popular scheme for the required feature extraction and classification tasks. The classical networks use diffusion-based smearing and block-wise pooling to downsample the image data and capture important structural features. In this work, we propose and demonstrate a more efficient quantum-inspired strategy involving a mixture of experts. It is a hybrid classical-quantum framework. The quantum part consists of amplitude encoding of the images, convolution using local unitary operations, multiple experts processing the same image with different parameters, and feature extraction using quantum stabiliser codes. The classical part then jointly processes the features extracted by different experts using a standard fully connected neural network for image class prediction. Using MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets as benchmarks, we demonstrate that the joint expert analysis outperforms the individual expert one, as well as reduces the failure rate of image class prediction by around a factor of two. The overhead of our quantum-inspired strategy is only moderate on GPU workstations, which makes our proposal a practical alternative to existing classical schemes. We also point out how the quantum part of our framework can be executed on a quantum processor.

</details>


### [63] [The Importance of Encoder Choice:A Tabular-Image Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07756)
*Ilia Koloiarov,Diego Coello de Portugal Mecke,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次在图像-表格多模态学习中评估最先进表格模型作为编码器的效果，解决了上下文学习模型依赖标签导致训练/测试实例嵌入不一致的问题，强调编码器选择对多模态学习的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态学习中表格模态多采用简单MLP作为编码器，但表格领域仍是深度学习的“最后未被攻克的堡垒”，表明MLP编码器不够强。作者旨在首次系统评估最先进表格模型作为编码器在图像-表格任务中的表现，并凸显编码器因素在多模态学习中的关键作用。

Method: 将最先进的表格模型作为编码器应用于图像-表格多模态学习。针对表现最好的上下文学习（ICL）模型需要标签才能处理实例、导致训练/测试嵌入方式不一致的难题，作者在该模型家族的多个模型中提出了解决方案，实现了统一的实例嵌入。

Result: 成功解决了上下文学习模型依赖标签的嵌入一致性问题，完成了多个最先进表格模型在图像-表格多模态任务中的编码器评估，验证了强表格编码器优于简单MLP的有效性。

Conclusion: 编码器的选择在多模态学习中至关重要。采用最先进的表格模型作为编码器能显著提升图像-表格多模态学习性能，打破了以往仅依赖简单MLP的局限，为表格模态在多模态学习中的表示提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal learning usually requires a dedicated encoder per modality. When a tabular modality is involved, prior work has been mostly using a \emph{plain MLP} as the encoder. Yet if it were a strong encoder, the tabular domain would not be ``the last unconquered castle for deep learning''. This study evaluates state-of-the-art tabular models as encoders in the image-tabular setting for the first time. An obstacle stands out. In-Context Learning models, among the best performing methods in the tabular domain, require labels to process instances, making it non-trivial to embed training and test instances the same way. We addressed this problem across multiple models of this family. With this study, we would like to highlight the importance of encoder factor in the multimodal learning.

</details>


### [64] [Scalable and Trustworthy Earth Observation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07758)
*Syed Usama Imtiaz,Mitra Nasr Azadani,Nasrin Alamdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本章综述了遥感基础模型（RSFM）的设计原则，强调领域特定约束（测量物理、运行决策）要求针对性适配；基准测试表明无单一模型通用最优，评估不一致是主要问题；提出下一代 RSFM 应按基准精度、模态感知迁移与物理合理表示三维评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星/机载档案数据量大、重访周期短、多模态丰富，但可靠现场标签稀缺；通用基础模型若不结合遥感领域的物理测量约束与运行决策约束，无法可靠/最优迁移，因此需要系统梳理领域指导的设计原则。

Method: 文献综述与综合分析：定义遥感基础模型范式，梳理模型全景、预训练目标、架构设计、下游适配与可信需求；汇总基准证据；提供两个环境监测案例研究——基于物理先验的光谱靶向掩码用于有害藻华预测、强化学习用于自适应监测站选址——演示领域指导原则的实践落地。

Result: 基准测试显示无单一地理空间基础模型在所有任务上通用最优，评估协议不一致阻碍公平比较与可靠部署；两个案例研究验证了物理感知掩码与强化学习选址能有效提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 下一代 RSFM 的评价体系应从单一基准精度扩展为三维：基准精度、模态感知迁移能力、物理合理表示能力，以支撑可信的地球观测决策。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have transformed machine learning from isolated task-specific model development toward general-purpose models pretrained on broad data and adapted to multiple downstream tasks. Earth observation (EO) is an important domain for this paradigm because satellite and airborne archives are large, high-revisit, and increasingly multimodal, while reliable field labels are often sparse. Remote sensing foundation models (RSFMs) cannot be transferred reliably/optimally without domain-specific adaptation. This is because EO data are governed by measurement physics and operational decision constraints. This chapter reviews the design principles arising from these domain-specific constraints. It first defines the FMs paradigm in remote sensing (RS), then synthesizes the current model landscape, pretraining objectives, architecture designs, downstream adaptation and trustworthiness requirements. The chapter also incorporates recent benchmark evidence showing that no single geospatial foundation model is universally best and that inconsistent evaluation remains a major issue to fair comparison and reliable deployment. In addition, two brief environmental monitoring case studies; physics-informed spectral targeted masking for harmful algal bloom prediction and reinforcement learning for adaptive environmental monitoring station selection to illustrate the FMs domain-guided principles in practice. This chapter posits that next-generation RSFMs should be evaluated not only by benchmark accuracy, but also by modality-aware transfer and physically plausible representations for trustworthy EO decisions.

</details>


### [65] [Trustworthy Machine Learning through the Lens of Combinatorial Optimization: Survey and Research Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07762)
*Thibaut Vidal,Julien Ferry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了组合优化（CO）在可信机器学习中的应用，涵盖可解释模型学习、解释生成、鲁棒性分析、公平性审计、模型压缩、隐私攻击与保护等任务。CO方法提供全局保证、形式化证书和显式权衡，虽面临可扩展性挑战，但求解器和混合算法的进步预示其在可信ML系统设计部署中作用日增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型日益复杂，仅凭经验性能指标难以全面刻画其行为；性能相似的模型在透明度、可解释性、鲁棒性、公平性、隐私性和可认证性上可能差异巨大。需要优化和认证导向的推理框架来支持模型训练、选择、审计和认证等任务。

Method: 综述并综合组合优化与可信ML交叉领域的最新进展，涵盖训练和训练后任务，包括可解释模型学习、解释生成、鲁棒性分析、公平性审计、模型压缩、隐私攻击与保护。分析CO公式相对于纯启发式方法（如基于梯度的方法）的优势：全局保证、形式化证书、显式权衡处理。

Result: CO公式在上述领域提供了额外能力，特别是全局最优性保证、形式化证书和显式权衡处理。尽管可扩展性仍是重要挑战，但求解器和混合算法的持续进步表明CO在可信ML系统设计和部署中的作用将日益增长。

Conclusion: 组合优化为可信机器学习提供了强有力的理论框架和工具，能够提供形式化保证和显式权衡，尽管面临规模化挑战，但随着求解技术进步，其在可信ML系统全生命周期中的应用前景广阔。

Abstract: Modern machine learning (ML) increasingly relies on complex models whose behavior is difficult to characterize beyond empirical performance metrics. Across a wide range of tasks, including prediction, generation, and decision-making, models with similar empirical performance can exhibit markedly different properties in terms of their transparency, interpretability, robustness, fairness, privacy, and certifiability. This survey highlights how optimization- and certification-oriented reasoning can provide a useful framework for reasoning about such differences, supporting tasks ranging from model training and selection to auditing and certification. We review and synthesize recent advances at the intersection of combinatorial optimization (CO) and trustworthy ML, covering both training and post-training tasks, including interpretable model learning, explanation generation, robustness analysis, fairness auditing, model compression, and privacy attacks and protections.
